Let's design a multi-start natural gradient/log-sum-exp algorithm for p length n (maybe 512/1024). It should be able to reduce score. We can also perform coordinate "symmetry breaking" by starting from exponential or random. We can evaluate exact score for best.

Detailed algorithm proposal:
1. set all helper functions top-level.
2. Function normalize(a, target_sum): a = max(a, floor); a = a/sum(a)*target_sum; return.
3. Function conv_fft(a): pad L=next_power_two(2n-1); P=fft(a, L); b=real(ifft(P*P)); clip small negatives; return b[:2n-1].
4. Function score_fft(a): if sum<0.01 return inf; normalize? If we keep sum target, max_b/?? exact score = 2n max_b / sum^2. But for search, normalize before.
5. Function softmax_weights(b, beta, top? maybe): subtract maxb for numeric stability; exp(beta*(b-maxb)); normalize. beta maybe = 20/(maxb - quantile?) or adaptive.

Natural gradient update:
```
def gradient_weights(p, b, mode='lse', beta=None):
    maxb = b.max()
    if beta is None:
       # temperature so top 10% weight? e.g. beta = log(K)/spread.
       # b spread ~ maxb - median.
       med = np.median(b)
       spread = maxb - med
       if spread <=0: beta=1*len(b)/maxb
       else: beta = 10.0 / spread
    w = np.exp(beta*(b - maxb))
    s = w.sum()
    if s<=0: w = np.zeros_like(b); w[argmax]=1
    else: w/=s
    return w
```

Detailed surrogate score:
```
def surrogate_score(p):
    p = np.asarray(p)
    s = p.sum()
    if s <= 1e-12: return np.inf
    q = p/s
    L = 1
    while L < 2*q.size-1: L <<=1
    P = np.fft.rfft(q, L)
    B = np.fft.irfft(P*P.conj()? Wait square: P*P, not conjugate, because ordinary convolution p*p, not autocorrelation. For real p, FFT of convolution p*p is FFT(p)*FFT(p). Not |P|^2. Yes use P*P.
    B = B[:2n-1]
    B = np.where(B>0,B,0) maybe tiny negative.
    return 2*n*B.max()
```
Because score with sum1 =2n max. Need if sum not1, formula 2n max/S^2. But we keep sum1.

The LSE weights from B.

Correlation v: We need v_i = ∑_k w_k p_{k-i}. Use N=2n-1. w length N. Compute rev_w = w[::-1]. Pad to L. R = rfft(rev_w, L). H = ifft(P * R). idx = N-1 - i for i=0..n-1. v=H[idx]. Since P from q (sum1). w sum1. v values? If w is a probability over k, v_i = E_k p_{k-i}. Good. It may have small imaginary, take real, clip0.

Multiplicative update:
```
def multiplicative_update(p, v, delta=0.5):
    centered = v - v.mean()
    scale = max(np.max(np.abs(centered)), 1e-300)
    eta = delta / scale
    logp = np.log(p + tiny) - eta*centered
    # To avoid under/overflow, subtract logp mean
    logp -= logp.mean()
    p_new = np.exp(logp)
    p_new = np.maximum(p_new, 1e-300)
    p_new /= p_new.sum()
    return p_new
```
But if p has zeros, log problem. We can avoid by floor: p_safe = p + tiny (then renormalize). But tiny may disturb. Use `np.log(np.maximum(p, 1e-300))`.

After update, mix: `p_trials = p + alpha*(p_new-p)` for alpha in [0.2,0.5,0.8,1.0]? Evaluate score. But if p_new very different, mix. Since p and p_new sum1, mix sum1. Choose best. If no improvement, try smaller/larger eta or weights.

Weight functions:
```
def weights_from_b(b, mode, param):
   N=b.size
   maxb=b.max()
   if mode == 'top':
       threshold = np.quantile(b, 1-param) maybe top fraction.
       w=(b>threshold).astype(float)
   elif 'exp':
       # temperature tau = param / (maxb - np.median(b))
       w=np.exp((b - maxb)/tau)
   else 'power':
       w=np.maximum(b - (1-alpha)*maxb,0)
```

Algorithm loop details:
```
def optimize_p(p, n, budget, rng, start_best):
    best = p.copy(); best_s = score_fft(p)
    b = conv_fft(p)
    st = 0
    for method in cycles:
       # choose weights
       w = make_weights(b, mode, beta)
       v = corr(p,w)
       # candidate
       p2 = exp_update(p,v, eta)
       # line search alphas
       for a in alphas:
           trial = a*p2+(1-a)*p; trial=sum1positive
           s = score_fft(trial)
           if s<best_s:
               best=trial; best_s=s; b=conv_fft(best)
    # occasional random perturb
```

def safe_eval(seq):
    try:
       val = evaluate_sequence(seq)
    except Exception:
       val = np.inf
    if val is None: return np.inf
    return float(val)
```

Detailed implement of weights:
```
def make_weights(b, mode, seed):
    N=b.size
    maxb=b.max()
    if maxb <=0: uniform
    if mode == 0: # exp temp
        ref = maxb - np.median(b)
        if ref <= 0: ref = maxb*0.1
        beta = 10.0 / ref
        w = np.exp(beta*(b-maxb))
    elif mode == 1: # top fraction
        q = np.quantile(b, 0.7)
        w = (b >= q).astype(float)
    elif mode == 2: # power above threshold
        q = maxb*0.6
        w = np.maximum(b-q,0)
    add uniform: w += 1e-4; w/=sum
```
We can cycle modes.

Multiplicative update eta: Use centered v. Scale = max(abs(v-min)) maybe. If scale=0 skip. eta = step_size / scale. step_size maybe 0.3 (factor e^0.3=1.35) or 0.8. We can try multiple eta values [0.2,0.5,1.0] each iteration; choose best. That increases FFT cost but okay.

Because p values normalized, if v has large variance, centered v scale could be O(max p) ~0.01? eta large. But if p has huge spike p0=0.1, v scale maybe0.1, eta ~1. Good.

def start_geometric(n, c, rev):
   x=np.linspace(0,1,n)
   if rev: x=1-x
   p=np.exp(-c*x)
   p=p+p.min()*1e-6
   p/=p.sum()
   return p
```

```
def normalize_prob(p):
    p=np.asarray(p,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    p=np.where(np.isfinite(p), p, 0.0)
    p=np.clip(p, 0.0, 1e100)
    p=np.maximum(p, 1e-300)
    s=p.sum()
    if not finite s or s<=1e-300: return np.ones(len(p))/len(p)
    return p/s
```
But floor 1e-300 may underflow; use 1e-15 for n up to 10000? 1e-15 sum negligible. Use 1e-14? If n=8192, sum floor8e-11 negligible. Good. `p = np.maximum(p, 1e-14); p/=p.sum()`. - `def exact_score(seq):` call evaluate_sequence.
```
def exact_score(seq):
    try:
       val=evaluate_sequence(seq)
    except Exception:
       return np.inf
    if val is None or not np.isfinite(val): return np.inf
    return float(val)
```

- `def make_weights(b, mode, rng):`
```
def make_weights(b, mode, rng):
    N=b.size; maxb=b.max()
    if not finite or maxb<=0: return np.ones(N)/N
    if mode == 0:
       med=np.median(b)
       ref=maxb-med
       if ref <= maxb*1e-6: ref=maxb*0.05
       beta=8.0/ref
       x=beta*(b-maxb)
       x=x - x.max() # already ≤0
       w=np.exp(x)
    elif mode == 1:
       threshold=np.quantile(b, 0.8) # top20
       w=(b>=threshold).astype(float)
       if w.sum()==0: w=np.zeros(N); idx=np.argmax(b); w[idx]=1
    elif mode == 2:
       threshold=maxb*0.7
       w=np.maximum(b-threshold,0)
       if w.sum()==0: uniform? 
    else:
       top=64
       idx=np.argpartition(b, N-top)[-top:]; w=...
    w += 1e-4 * (w.sum()/N?) or just w += 1e-5
    s=w.sum(); if s>0: w/=s else uniform
    return w
```
Uniform component: `w = (1-0.01)*w + 0.01*np.ones(N)/N`; if w originally sum1. Use `w = w + 1e-4; w/=w.sum()`.

- `def multiplicative_step(p, v, delta):`
```
centered=v-v.mean()
scale=np.max(np.abs(centered))
if scale <= 1e-300: return None
eta=delta/scale
logp=np.log(p)-eta*centered
# center logp to avoid overflow
logp=logp-logp.mean()
# clip logp to [-30,30]
logp=np.clip(logp, -30, 30)
pn=np.exp(logp)
pn=np.maximum(pn, 1e-18)
pn/=pn.sum()
return pn
```

def perturb_p(p, rng, strength=0.2):
   n=p.size
   cand=p.copy()
   # several blocks
   for _ in range(3):
      width=max(2, n//64)
      start=int(rng.integers(0,n-width+1))
      factor=np.exp(rng.normal(0,strength))
      cand[start:start+width]*=factor
   # sometimes global asymmetry: multiply by exp(rng.normal(0,0.2)*x)
   if rng.random()<0.3:
      x=np.linspace(0,1,n)
      cand *= np.exp(rng.normal(0,0.3)*x)
   return normalize_prob(cand)
```

def sample_global(n, rng):

       g = np.asarray(height_sequence_1, dtype=np.float64)

    x_old=np.linspace(0,1,g.size)
    x_new=np.linspace(0,1,n)

def optimize_p(p,n,stop,rng,do_lp=False):
    best=normalize_prob(p); current=best.copy()
    best_s=score_prob(best); cur_s=best_s
    b=conv_fft(best)
    wmode=0
    stall=0
    lp_count=0
    it=0
    while time<stop and it<50000:
       it+=1
       # Choose current = best if too far
       if cur_s > best_s*1.03 + 1e-4:
           current=best.copy(); cur_s=best_s; b=conv_fft(current)
       # weights modes cycle
       mode = wmode % 3; wmode +=1
       w=make_weights(b, mode, rng)
       v=corr_weights(current,w)
       for delta in (0.35,0.9):
           pn=multiplicative_step(current,v,delta)
           if pn is None: continue
           scores=[]

           s=score_prob(cand)
           if s<cur_s:

           if s<best_s*(1-1e-7): best=cand; best_s=s; b=conv_fft(cand); current=cand; cur_s=s; stall=0
           # alpha=0.5 if not update?

              current=cand2; cur_s=s2; b=conv_fft(cand2)

           time check
       if it%250 == 0 and stall>0:

           s=score_prob(cand)
           if s<best_s: update
       if it%1000==0 and do_lp and n<=1536 and timeleft>20 and lp_count<3:
           g=lp_direction(best,n)
           if g is not None:
              for alpha... update
           lp_count +=1
       if it % 500 ==0: print
    return best, best_s
```

LP direction function must be optional; if scipy import fails, skip. Let's code `lp_direction(p,n)` using sparse diags. Need ensure SciPy available. If no, return None.
```
def lp_direction(p, n):
    if not HAVE_SCIPY: return None
    S=np.sqrt(2.0*n)
    f=p*S
    floor=1e-7*S
    f=np.maximum(f, floor)
    f=f/f.sum()*S
    b=conv_fft(f)
    M=b.max()
    if not finite M or M<=0: return None
    # build C
    try:
       # scipy.sparse.diags with [f]*n may be memory heavy; if n>2048 skip
       if n>2048: return None
       C=sparse.diags([f.copy() for _ in range(n)], offsets=np.arange(n), shape=(2*n-1,n), format='csc')
       c=-np.ones(n)
       res=linprog(c, A_ub=C, b_ub=np.full(2*n-1, M), bounds=[(0,None)]*n, method='highs', options={'presolve': True})
    except Exception:
       return None
    if not res.success: return None
    g=res.x
    if g is None: return None
    g=np.asarray(g,dtype=float)
    g=np.where(np.isfinite(g),g,0)
    g=np.maximum(g,0)
    gs=g.sum()
    if gs<=0: return None
    g=(S/gs)*g
    pg=g/S
    pg=normalize_prob(pg)
    return pg
```
But `bounds=[(0,None)]*n` creates list of same tuple okay. For n=2048, okay. `sparse.diags([f.copy() for _ in range(n)], ...)` list of 2048 refs; memory maybe list small but each copy 2048 floats *2048=32MB plus sparse 32MB. okay. Could use `[f] * n` no copy; sparse will copy? It may copy. Use `[f] * n` to reduce list memory; but `f` same object, okay. If SciPy modifies? It converts. Use `[f] * n`.

However `sparse.diags([f]*n, offsets=np.arange(n), shape=(2*n-1,n))` for offset 0 length min? It might expect len n for offset0; offset positive d length n? As reasoned, length n. `conv_fft`:
```
def conv_fft(a):
    a=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64)
    n=a.size
    if n==0: return np.empty(0)
    L=1
    need=2*n-1
    while L<need: L <<=1
    A=np.fft.rfft(a,L)
    B=np.fft.irfft(A*A,L, n=L)[:need]
    B=np.real(B)
    # remove tiny negative
    B[B<0] = 0.0? This modifies copy okay.
    return B
```
If A*A large, fine. Note if a all zero, B zeros.

m=b.max()
return float(2*p.size*m)

```
def make_weights(b, mode, rng):
    N=b.size; maxb=b.max()
    if maxb <= 1e-300: return ones/N
    mean=b.mean()
    if mode==0:
       ref=max(1e-300, maxb - mean)
       beta=8.0/ref
       x=beta*(b-maxb)
       w=np.exp(x)
    elif mode==1:
       thr = maxb - 0.35*(maxb - mean)
       w = np.where(b >= thr, 1.0, 0.0)
       if w.sum()==0: w=...
    elif mode==2:
       thr=0.6*maxb
       w=np.maximum(b-thr,0.0)
       if w.sum()<1e-300: w=uniform? or argmax

`multiplicative_step`: Need p positive. We normalized p floor. Use log. If delta large, clip.
```
def multiplicative_step(p, v, delta):
    v=v.astype(float)
    centered=v-v.mean()
    scale=np.max(np.abs(centered))
    if scale <= 1e-300: return None
    eta=delta/scale
    logp=np.log(p) - eta*centered
    logp = logp - logp.mean()
    logp = np.clip(logp, -40.0, 40.0)
    pn=np.exp(logp)
    pn=np.maximum(pn, _FLOOR)
    pn/=pn.sum()
    return pn
```

Detailed functions:
```
def fft_len(n):
    need=2*n-1
    L=1
    while L<need: L <<=1
    return L
```

`conv_fft` with A*A. Since `A` complex128, `A*A` okay. `irfft` expects length L; if A length L//2+1; A*A same length. Good.

`score_fast`:
```
def score_fast(p):
    if p.size ==0: return np.inf
    s=p.sum()
    if not finite or s<=0: return np.inf
    # if not normalized, adjust: score=2n max(conv(p))/s^2 *? conv scales s^2. Our p normalized but candidate mix might slightly; better use formula for robustness.
    b=conv_fft(p)
    m=b.max()
    if not finite or m<=0: return np.inf
    s2=s*s
    return float(2*p.size*m/s2)
```
This handles unnormalized. Extra sum O(n), okay. But if p normalized, no issue. Use this. For many calls, sum O(n) negligible vs FFT. Good.

`normalize_prob` with `p = np.asanyarray` ensures 1D. If p length 0, return ones(1)? But n fixed. If p contains NaN, set 0.
```
def normalize_prob(p):
    p=np.asarray(p,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if p.size==0: return np.ones(1)
    p=np.where(np.isfinite(p),p,0.0)
    p=np.maximum(p,0.0)
    p=np.maximum(p,1e-18)
    s=p.sum()
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s<=0: return np.full_like(p,1.0/p.size)
    return p/s
```

def exact_score(seq):
   try: val=evaluate_sequence(seq)
   except Exception: return np.inf
   try: val=float(val)
   except: return np.inf
   if not np.isfinite(val): return np.inf
   return val

`optimize_p` pseudocode with code details:
```
def optimize_p(p, n, stop_time, rng, do_lp=False):
    p=normalize_prob(np.asarray(p,dtype=float))
    best=p.copy(); best_s=score_fast(p)
    current=p.copy(); cur_s=best_s
    b=conv_fft(current)
    lp_count=0
    it=0
    max_iter=50000
    styles=[0,1,2,3,4]
    while time.monotonic() < stop_time and it < max_iter:
        it +=1
        # LP steps first
        if do_lp and n <= 1024 and lp_count < 4 and stop_time - time.monotonic() > 15:
            d=lp_direction(best,n,stop_time)
            if d is not None:
                best_trial=best; best_trial_s=best_s
                for a in (0.1,0.3,0.6,1.0):
                    cand=(1-a)*best + a*d
                    cand=normalize_prob(cand)
                    s=score_fast(cand)
                    if s < best_trial_s:
                        best_trial=cand; best_trial_s=s
                if best_trial_s < best_s:
                    best=best_trial; best_s=best_trial_s; current=best.copy(); cur_s=best_s; b=conv_fft(current)
                lp_count +=1
                continue
        if cur_s > best_s * 1.04 + 1e-5:
            current=best.copy(); cur_s=best_s; b=conv_fft(current)
        style = it % 5
        w=make_weights(b, style, rng)
        v=corr_weights(current,w)
        if v.size==0: continue
        for delta in (0.35, 0.9):
            pn=multiplicative_step(current, v, delta)
            if pn is None: continue
            # evaluate full and half
            for alpha in (1.0, 0.5):
                cand=pn if alpha>=1 else (1-alpha)*current + alpha*pn
                cand=normalize_prob(cand)
                s=score_fast(cand)
                if s < cur_s:
                    current=cand; cur_s=s; b=conv_fft(cand)
                if s < best_s * (1.0 - 1e-9):
                    best=cand.copy(); best_s=s; b=conv_fft(best); current=best.copy(); cur_s=s
                if time.monotonic() >= stop_time: return best, best_s
        # swap move every 150 iters
        if it % 150 == 0:
            v2=corr_weights(current, make_weights(current if False else b, style, rng), rng)? We can reuse v maybe stale. Use v.
            hi=int(np.argmax(v)); lo=int(np.argmin(v))
            if v[hi] > v[lo] * 1.01 and p[hi] > _FLOOR:
                for frac in (0.02,0.05):
                    cand=current.copy(); delta=frac*cand[hi]
                    cand[hi]-=delta; cand[lo]+=delta
                    cand=normalize_prob(cand)
                    s=score_fast(cand)
                    if s<cur_s: current=cand...
                    if s<best_s: best=...
        if it % 1000 == 0: print(...)
    return best,best_s
```

```
def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=400, **kwargs):
    t0=time.monotonic(); deadline=t0+max(1.0,float(budget_s))-2.0
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
    # fallback uniform
    nbase=256
    pbase=np.ones(nbase)/nbase
    seqbase=[float(x) for x in pbase]
    best_exact=exact_score(seqbase)
    if not np.isfinite(best_exact) or best_exact>2.1:
       best_exact=2.0
    best_seq=seqbase
    sur_best_p=None; sur_best_n=1; sur_best_s=np.inf; sur_checked_s=np.inf
    
    g = globals().get('height_sequence_1', None)
    global_seq = None
    if g is not None:
       try:
          arr=np.asarray(g,dtype=np.float64).ravel(); arr=np.clip(np.where(np.isfinite(arr),arr,0),0,1e6)
          if arr.size>0 and arr.sum()>0: global_seq=arr
       except: global_seq=None
    
    phases=[]
    if budget_s >= 150:
       phases=[(512,0.08,True),(1024,0.27,True),(2048,0.30,False),(4096,0.25,False),(8192,0.10,False)]
    elif budget_s >=60:
       phases=[(1024,0.45,True),(2048,0.35,False),(4096,0.20,False)]
    else:
       phases=[(1024,0.6,False),(2048,0.4,False)]
    
    for n, frac, do_lp in phases:
        stop = min(deadline, time.monotonic() + frac*float(budget_s))
        if stop - time.monotonic() < 5: continue
        idx=0
        min_alloc= max(2.0, min(45.0, (stop-time.monotonic())/6.0))
        while time.monotonic() < stop - 2.0:
            seg_stop = min(stop - 1.0, time.monotonic() + min_alloc)
            # dynamic seg_stop maybe if remaining small
            if seg_stop - time.monotonic() < 1.0: break
            p0=make_start(n, rng, idx, global_seq)
            pbest, sbest = optimize_p(p0, n, seg_stop, rng, do_lp=do_lp)
            if np.isfinite(sbest) and sbest < sur_best_s:
                sur_best_s=sbest; sur_best_p=pbest.copy(); sur_best_n=nbest; sur_checked_s=np.inf
                # exact check if can beat best_exact by surrogate margin or close
                if sbest < best_exact + 0.05 and time.monotonic() < deadline - 3.0:
                    seq=[float(x) for x in sur_best_p]
                    es=exact_score(seq)
                    if np.isfinite(es) and es < best_exact:
                       best_exact=es; best_seq=seq
                       print("New exact best", best_exact, "n", n)
                    sur_checked_s=sbest
            idx +=1
            # adjust min_alloc? if many quick
            if idx % 4 ==0:
                min_alloc = max(2.0, min(30.0, (stop-time.monotonic())/4.0))
        # after phase, exact check sur if not checked
    # Final exact check sur_best if not checked and time
    if sur_best_p is not None and sur_best_s < sur_checked_s:
        seq=[float(x) for x in sur_best_p]
        es=exact_score(seq)
        if np.isfinite(es) and es < best_exact:
            best_exact=es; best_seq=seq
    # If best_seq still base? maybe exact check sur until best.
    final_seq = best_seq if isinstance(best_seq,list) else [float(x) for x in best_seq]
    # Ensure finite positive
    final_seq = [float(max(0.0, x)) for x in final_seq if np.isfinite(x)]
    if not final_seq: final_seq=[1.0]
    return final_seq
```

```
def make_start(n, rng, idx, global_seq):
    x=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,n)
    kind=idx % 12
    rev = (idx % 2)==1
    xx = 1.0 - x if rev else x
    c = 1.0 + 0.8*(idx % 4)  # 1 to3.4
    if kind == 0 or kind == 4:
       p=np.exp(-c*xx)
    elif kind == 1:
       p=np.exp(-2.0*xx - 0.2*np.sin(2*np.pi*xx))
    elif kind == 2:
       p=1.0 - 0.5*xx + 0.25*np.sin(2*np.pi*xx)
    elif kind == 3:
       p=1.0 - 0.8*xx + 0.2*np.cos(2*np.pi*xx)
    elif kind == 5:
       p=1.0 + 0.7*np.sin(2*np.pi*xx + 0.3)
    elif kind == 6:
       p=1.0 + 0.8*np.sin(2*np.pi*xx + 1.7) + 0.4*np.sin(4*np.pi*xx + 0.5)
    elif kind == 7:
       p=np.exp(-c*xx) * (1.0 + 0.3*np.sin(2*np.pi*xx))
    elif kind == 8:
       p=rng.gamma(2.0,1.0,n); p *= np.exp(rng.uniform(-0.5,0.5)*xx)
    elif kind == 9:
       y=np.zeros(n)
       for j in range(1,5):
          phase=rng.uniform(0,2*np.pi)
          y += rng.uniform(-1.0,1.0)*np.cos(2*np.pi*j*xx+phase)
       p=np.exp(0.9*y)
    elif kind == 10:
       p=np.exp(-0.5*xx) * np.exp(0.2*np.sin(4*np.pi*xx))
    else:
       p=np.ones(n) * (1.0 + 0.1*np.random.normal?) # no random state? use rng
       p = n / (np.maximum(1, xx)+1)?
```
Need avoid negative for sin/cos: use np.maximum(p,1e-3). For gamma positive. For kind 11 maybe `p=1/(1+xx*3)`.

For global start, separate.

Ensure p after `np.maximum` not all zero. Use `_floor_start=1e-4` to avoid too small. `normalize_prob` floors to1e-18 anyway, but initial floor 1e-3 may reduce asymmetry if p alternates sign? If p has negative regions clipped to1e-4, okay.

Now, exact_polish function:
```
def exact_polish(p,n,stop_time,rng):
    p=normalize_prob(p); cur=exact_score([...]); best=p.copy(); best_e=cur
    b=conv_fft(p)
    it=0
    while time<stop and it<2000:
       it+=1
       style=it%5; w=make_weights(b,style,rng); v=corr_weights(p,w)
       for delta in (0.2,0.5):
          pn=multiplicative_step(p,v,delta)
          if pn is None: continue
          # choose best surrogate among full/half
          candidates=[pn, 0.5*p+0.5*pn]
          # evaluate surrogate quickly? maybe use score_fast to reject if worse than best_e - 1e-5
          for cand in candidates:
             cand=normalize_prob(cand)
             s_sur=score_fast(cand)
             if s_sur < best_e - 1e-6:
                 es=exact_score([float(x) for x in cand])
                 if np.isfinite(es) and es < cur:
                    p=cand; cur=es; b=conv_fft(p)
                 if es < best_e:
                    best=cand.copy(); best_e=es
       if it%50==0:
          hi=int(np.argmax(v)); lo=int(np.argmin(v)) ... exact swap
    return best,best_e
```
But using `best_e` as threshold and cur; if exact close to surrogate. Good. Limit if n>4096 skip or fewer.

In exact_polish, if exact_score is inf for cand due? skip.

Now, final call exact_polish if time left and n≤4096. If n=8192 maybe skip to avoid exact heavy, but could use surrogate only. We can still exact check once. Use if n≤4096.

Now, possible issue: `exact_polish` uses `best_e` for surrogate threshold; if exact best 2 and best_e variable, good.

`multipliers`: If `v` centered scale tiny, return None; but if p uniform, no update. Perturb.

If LP direction not improve best but improves current? We only best_trial based on best, not current. Maybe skip. But LP from best is better.

In optimize inner, when no improvement for long, `current` may drift. If `cur_s > best_s*1.04`, reset. Good.

```python
"""
Nonnegative step-function search.

The code searches probability-like vectors p >= 0, sum(p) = 1, minimizing
    2*n*max_k (p*p)_k
which is equivalent to the evaluator score.  It uses:
- FFT-based convolution for fast surrogate objective values,
- multiplicative natural-gradient steps with several coefficient-weighting styles,
- occasional coordinate mass swaps,
- optional HiGHS LP best-response directions, inspired by the previous SOTA,
- multiple starts/lengths, and final exact polishing.
"""

import numpy as np
import time

try:
    from scipy.optimize import linprog
    from scipy import sparse
    _HAVE_SCIPY = True
except Exception:
    _HAVE_SCIPY = False

_INT_FLOOR = 1e-18


def fft_len(n):
    """Smallest FFT power of two that avoids circular wraparound."""
    need = 2 * int(n) - 1
    L = 1
    while L < need:
        L <<= 1
    return L


def normalize_prob(p):
    """Make a finite nonnegative vector with sum 1."""
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if p.size == 0:
        return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    p = np.where(np.isfinite(p), p, 0.0)
    p = np.clip(p, 0.0, 1e100)
    p = np.maximum(p, _INT_FLOOR)
    s = float(p.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        return np.full(p.shape, 1.0 / p.size, dtype=np.float64)
    p /= s
    return p


def conv_fft(a):
    """Exact linear convolution a*a by FFT, returned as real nonnegative."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)
    need = 2 * n - 1
    L = fft_len(n)
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, L)
    B = np.fft.irfft(A * A, L)[:need]
    B = np.real(B)
    B = np.maximum(B, 0.0)
    return B


def score_fast(p):
    """Score for any p, using the exact scaling-invariant formula."""
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = p.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.inf
    s = float(p.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        return np.inf
    b = conv_fft(p)
    m = float(b.max()) if b.size else 0.0
    if not np.isfinite(m) or m < 0.0:
        return np.inf
    return float(2.0 * n * m / (s * s))


def exact_score(seq):
    """Safely call the externally provided evaluate_sequence."""
    try:
        val = evaluate_sequence(seq)
    except Exception:
        return np.inf
    try:
        val = float(val)
    except Exception:
        return np.inf
    if not np.isfinite(val):
        return np.inf
    return val


def make_weights(b, style, rng):
    """Create probability weights over convolution coefficients.

    Different styles correspond to different L-infinity approximations.
    """
    b = np.asarray(b, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    N = b.size
    if N == 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)
    maxb = float(b.max())
    mean = float(b.mean())
    if not np.isfinite(maxb) or maxb <= 0.0:
        return np.full(N, 1.0 / N, dtype=np.float64)

    ref = maxb - mean
    if not np.isfinite(ref) or ref <= 1e-300:
        ref = maxb * 0.05 + 1e-300

    style = int(style) % 5
    if style == 0:
        beta = 3.0 / ref
        w = np.exp(beta * (b - maxb))
    elif style == 1:
        beta = 12.0 / ref
        w = np.exp(beta * (b - maxb))
    elif style == 2:
        beta = 40.0 / ref
        w = np.exp(beta * (b - maxb))
    elif style == 3:
        thr = maxb - 0.35 * ref
        w = np.where(b >= thr, 1.0, 0.0)
    else:
        w = np.maximum(b - 0.50 * maxb, 0.0)

    # Small uniform floor keeps entropy/directions well defined.
    w = np.where(np.isfinite(w), np.maximum(w, 0.0), 0.0)
    w = w + 1e-4
    sw = float(w.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(sw) or sw <= 0.0:
        return np.full(N, 1.0 / N, dtype=np.float64)
    w /= sw
    return w


def corr_weights(p, w):
    """Compute v_i = sum_j p_j w_{i+j} efficiently by FFT."""
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    w = np.asarray(w, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = p.size
    if n == 0 or w.size == 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)
    Nneed = 2 * n - 1
    if w.size < Nneed:
        # Should not happen, but pad defensively.
        ww = np.zeros(Nneed, dtype=np.float64)
        m = min(w.size, Nneed)
        ww[:m] = w[:m]
        w = ww
    else:
        w = w[:Nneed]

    L = fft_len(n)
    P = np.fft.rfft(p, L)
    R = np.fft.rfft(w[::-1], L)
    H = np.fft.irfft(P * R, L)
    v = H[n - 1:Nneed][::-1].real
    v = np.maximum(v, 0.0)
    v = np.where(np.isfinite(v), v, 0.0)
    return v


def multiplicative_step(p, v, delta):
    """Natural exponential step on the simplex toward smaller v."""
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    v = np.asarray(v, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if p.size == 0 or v.size != p.size:
        return None
    centered = v - float(v.mean())
    scale = float(np.max(np.abs(centered)))
    if not np.isfinite(scale) or scale <= 1e-300:
        return None
    eta = float(delta) / scale
    logp = np.log(np.maximum(p, _INT_FLOOR)) - eta * centered
    logp = logp - float(logp.mean())
    logp = np.clip(logp, -40.0, 40.0)
    pn = np.exp(logp)
    pn = np.maximum(pn, _INT_FLOOR)
    pn = normalize_prob(pn)
    return pn


def sample_global_to_n(g, n):
    """Resample the supplied known construction to length n as a probability."""
    try:
        g = np.asarray(g, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    except Exception:
        return None
    if g.size < 2:
        return None
    g = np.where(np.isfinite(g), g, 0.0)
    g = np.maximum(g, 0.0)
    if float(g.sum()) <= 0.0:
        return None
    x_old = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, g.size)
    x_new = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, int(n))
    h = np.interp(x_new, x_old, g)
    h = np.maximum(h, 1e-4)
    h = normalize_prob(h)
    return h


def make_start(n, rng, idx, global_seq):
    """Generate diverse asymmetric nonnegative starting points."""
    n = int(n)
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)

    if global_seq is not None and (idx % 5 == 0):
        base = sample_global_to_n(global_seq, n)
        if base is not None:
            # Small asymmetric tilt removes artificial reversal symmetry.
            t = float(rng.uniform(-0.15, 0.15))
            base *= np.exp(t * x)
            return normalize_prob(base)

    kind = idx % 12
    rev = (idx % 2) == 1
    xx = 1.0 - x if rev else x
    c = 1.0 + 0.7 * float(idx % 4) + 0.1 * float(rng.random())

    if kind == 0:
        p = np.exp(-c * xx)
    elif kind == 1:
        p = np.exp(-1.8 * xx - 0.35 * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * xx))
    elif kind == 2:
        p = 1.0 - 0.55 * xx + 0.22 * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * xx + 0.4)
    elif kind == 3:
        p = 1.0 - 0.85 * xx + 0.18 * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * xx + 1.1)
    elif kind == 4:
        p = np.exp(-2.3 * xx) * (1.0 + 0.25 * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * xx))
    elif kind == 5:
        p = 1.0 + 0.65 * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * xx + 0.35)
    elif kind == 6:
        p = 1.0 + 0.55 * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * xx + 1.6) \
            + 0.30 * np.sin(4.0 * np.pi * xx + 0.7)
    elif kind == 7:
        p = rng.gamma(2.0, 1.0, n)
        t = float(rng.uniform(-0.6, 0.6))
        p *= np.exp(t * xx)
    elif kind == 8:
        y = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
        for j in range(1, 6):
            phase = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi))
            amp = float(rng.uniform(-1.0, 1.0))
            y += amp * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * j * xx + phase) / float(j)
        p = np.exp(0.9 * y)
    elif kind == 9:
        p = np.exp(-0.55 * xx) * np.exp(0.25 * np.sin(4.0 * np.pi * xx))
    elif kind == 10:
        p = 1.0 / (1.0 + 3.0 * xx)
        p *= 1.0 + 0.15 * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * xx + 0.9)
    else:
        p = np.exp(-c * xx)

    p = np.where(np.isfinite(p), p, 0.0)
    p = np.maximum(p, 1e-4)
    return normalize_prob(p)


def lp_direction(p, n, stop_time):
    """LP best-response direction using HiGHS, if available and cheap enough."""
    if not _HAVE_SCIPY:
        return None
    n = int(n)
    if n > 1024:
        return None
    left = stop_time - time.monotonic()
    if left < 4.0:
        return None

    p = normalize_prob(p)
    S = float(np.sqrt(2.0 * n))
    f = p * S
    floor_f = 1e-7 * S
    f = np.maximum(f, floor_f)
    f = normalize_prob(f) * S

    Mb = conv_fft(f)
    M = float(Mb.max())
    if not np.isfinite(M) or M <= 0.0:
        return None

    # C has row k, col i equal to f_i when i+j=k with 0 <= i,j < n.
    try:
        C = sparse.diags([f] * n, offsets=np.arange(n), shape=(2 * n - 1, n),
                         format="csc")
    except Exception:
        return None

    c = -np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    b_ub = np.full(2 * n - 1, M, dtype=np.float64)
    bounds = [(0.0, None)] * n

    timelimit = float(max(0.5, min(8.0, left - 1.5)))
    res = None
    option_sets = (
        {"presolve": True, "time_limit": timelimit},
        {"presolve": True},
    )
    for opts in option_sets:
        try:
            res = linprog(c, A_ub=C, b_ub=b_ub, bounds=bounds,
                          method="highs", options=options_opts(opts))
            break
        except Exception:
            res = None

    if res is None or not getattr(res, "success", False):
        return None
    g = getattr(res, "x", None)
    if g is None:
        return None
    g = np.asarray(g, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    g = np.where(np.isfinite(g), g, 0.0)
    g = np.maximum(g, 0.0)
    gs = float(g.sum())
    if gs <= 0.0:
        return None
    g *= S / gs
    pg = normalize_prob(g / S)
    return pg


def options_opts(d):
    return d


def optimize_p(p, n, stop_time, rng, do_lp=False):
    """Local search over a fixed length n. Returns best probability vector."""
    n = int(n)
    p = normalize_prob(p)
    best = p.copy()
    best_s = score_fast(best)
    current = p.copy()
    cur_s = best_s
    b = conv_fft(current)

    it = 0
    lp_count = 0
    max_iter = 60000

    while time.monotonic() < stop_time and it < max_iter:
        it += 1

        # Periodic LP directions from the global best.
        if do_lp and n <= 1024 and lp_count < 4 and stop_time - time.monotonic() > 20.0:
            d = lp_direction(best, n, stop_time)
            if d is not None:
                best_trial = best
                best_trial_s = best_s
                for a in (0.1, 0.3, 0.6, 1.0):
                    cand = (1.0 - a) * best + a * d
                    cand = normalize_prob(cand)
                    s = score_fast(cand)
                    if s < best_trial_s:
                        best_trial = cand
                        best_trial_s = s
                if best_trial_s < best_s:
                    best = best_trial.copy()
                    best_s = best_trial_s
                    current = best.copy()
                    cur_s = best_s
                    b = conv_fft(current)
                lp_count += 1
                continue

        # If the current trajectory is too far from the best, snap back.
        if cur_s > best_s * 1.04 + 1e-5:
            current = best.copy()
            cur_s = best_s
            b = conv_fft(current)

        style = it % 5
        w = make_weights(b, style, rng)
        v = corr_weights(current, w)
        if v.size == 0:
            continue

        # Two natural-gradient scales.
        for delta in (0.35, 0.90):
            pn = multiplicative_step(current, v, delta)
            if pn is None:
                continue
            cand_full = normalize_prob(pn)
            cand_half = normalize_prob(0.5 * current + 0.5 * pn)

            chosen = current
            chosen_s = cur_s
            for cand in (cand_full, cand_half):
                s = score_fast(cand)
                if np.isfinite(s) and s < chosen_s:
                    chosen = cand
                    chosen_s = s
            if chosen_s < cur_s:
                current = chosen
                cur_s = chosen_s
                b = conv_fft(current)
            if chosen_s < best_s * (1.0 - 1e-9):
                best = chosen.copy()
                best_s = chosen_s
                current = best.copy()
                cur_s = best_s
                b = conv_fft(current)

            if time.monotonic() >= stop_time:
                return best, best_s

        # Occasional direct coordinate swap guided by the current gradient.
        if it % 150 == 0:
            hi = int(np.argmax(v)) if v.size else 0
            lo = int(np.argmin(v)) if v.size else 0
            if hi == lo:
                lo = int(rng.integers(0, n))
            if hi != lo and v[hi] > 1.01 * v[lo]:
                for frac in (0.02, 0.06):
                    cand = current.copy()
                    dmass = frac * cand[hi]
                    if dmass <= _INT_FLOOR:
                        continue
                    cand[hi] -= dmass
                    cand[lo] += dmass
                    cand = normalize_prob(cand)
                    s = score_fast(cand)
                    if s < cur_s:
                        current = cand
                        cur_s = s
                        b = conv_fft(current)
                    if s < best_s:
                        best = cand.copy()
                        best_s = s
                        current = best.copy()
                        cur_s = s
                        b = conv_fft(current)

        if it % 4000 == 0:
            print("  n={:d} opt iter={:d} local_best={:.8f}".format(
                n, it, best_s), flush=True)

    return best, best_s


def exact_polish(p, n, stop_time, rng):
    """Very final exact-evaluator polishing; only for moderate lengths."""
    n = int(n)
    p = normalize_prob(p)
    cur_e = exact_score([float(x) for x in p])
    if not np.isfinite(cur_e):
        return p, np.inf
    best = p.copy()
    best_e = cur_e
    b = conv_fft(p)
    it = 0

    while time.monotonic() < stop_time and it < 2500:
        it += 1
        style = it % 5
        w = make_weights(b, style, rng)
        v = corr_weights(p, w)
        if v.size == 0:
            continue

        for delta in (0.25, 0.55):
            pn = multiplicative_step(p, v, delta)
            if pn is None:
                continue
            for cand in (normalize_prob(pn), normalize_prob(0.5 * p + 0.5 * pn)):
                # Use the fast score as a cheap filter before exact evaluation.
                s_sur = score_fast(cand)
                if s_sur >= best_e - 1e-7:
                    continue
                es = exact_score([float(x) for x in cand])
                if not np.isfinite(es):
                    continue
                if es < cur_e:
                    p = cand.copy()
                    cur_e = es
                    b = conv_fft(p)
                if es < best_e:
                    best = cand.copy()
                    best_e = es

        if it % 120 == 0:
            if v.size:
                hi = int(np.argmax(v))
                lo = int(np.argmin(v))
                if hi == lo:
                    lo = int(rng.integers(0, n))
                if hi != lo and v[hi] > 1.02 * v[lo]:
                    for frac in (0.02, 0.05):
                        cand = p.copy()
                        dm = frac * cand[hi]
                        if dm <= _INT_FLOOR:
                            continue
                        cand[hi] -= dm
                        cand[lo] += dm
                        cand = normalize_prob(cand)
                        es = exact_score([float(x) for x in cand])
                        if np.isfinite(es):
                            if es < cur_e:
                                p = cand.copy()
                                cur_e = es
                                b = conv_fft(p)
                            if es < best_e:
                                best = cand.copy()
                                best_e = es

    return best, best_e


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=400, **kwargs):
    """Main required entry point. Returns a list of nonnegative heights."""
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    budget = max(1.0, float(budget_s))
    deadline = t0 + budget - 3.0
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)

    # Guaranteed valid fallback: uniform vector has score exactly 2.
    nbase = 256
    pbase = np.ones(nbase, dtype=np.float64) / float(nbase)
    seqbase = [float(x) for x in pbase]
    best_exact = exact_score(seqbase)
    if not np.isfinite(best_exact) or best_exact > 2.5:
        best_exact = 2.0
    best_seq = seqbase

    # Best surrogate found overall, for final exact checks.
    sur_best_p = None
    sur_best_n = 1
    sur_best_s = np.inf
    sur_checked_s = np.inf

    # Optional known construction.
    global_seq = None
    g = globals().get("height_sequence_1", None)
    if g is not None:
        try:
            ga = np.asarray(g, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
            ga = np.where(np.isfinite(ga), ga, 0.0)
            ga = np.maximum(ga, 0.0)
            if ga.size > 1 and float(ga.sum()) > 0.0:
                global_seq = ga
        except Exception:
            global_seq = None

    print("Starting search, budget={:.1f}s".format(budget), flush=True)

    # Phase plan: moderate lengths first, then larger lengths for continuum accuracy.
    if budget >= 150.0:
        phases = [
            (512, 0.08, True),
            (1024, 0.27, True),
            (2048, 0.30, False),
            (4096, 0.25, False),
            (8192, 0.10, False),
        ]
    elif budget >= 60.0:
        phases = [
            (1024, 0.45, True),
            (2048, 0.35, False),
            (4096, 0.20, False),
        ]
    else:
        phases = [
            (1024, 0.60, False),
            (2048, 0.40, False),
        ]

    for n, frac, do_lp in phases:
        phase_stop = min(deadline, time.monotonic() + frac * budget)
        if phase_stop - time.monotonic() < 5.0:
            continue

        idx = 0
        # Adapt segment length so that time is not stranded in one long run.
        min_alloc = max(2.0, min(45.0, (phase_stop - time.monotonic()) / 6.0))

        while time.monotonic() < phase_stop - 2.0:
            seg_stop = min(phase_stop - 1.0, time.monotonic() + min_alloc)
            if seg_stop - time.monotonic() < 1.5:
                break

            p0 = make_start(n, rng, idx, global_seq)
            pbest, sbest = optimize_p(p0, n, seg_stop, rng, do_lp=do_lp)

            if np.isfinite(sbest) and sbest < sur_best_s:
                sur_best_s = sbest
                sur_best_p = pbest.copy()
                sur_best_n = n
                sur_checked_s = np.inf
                print("surrogate best: n={:d} score={:.8f}".format(n, sbest), flush=True)

                # Exact-check promising new surrogate bests.
                if sbest < best_exact + 0.05 and time.monotonic() < deadline - 4.0:
                    seq = [float(x) for x in sur_best_p]
                    es = exact_score(seq)
                    if np.isfinite(es) and es < best_exact:
                        best_exact = es
                        best_seq = seq
                        print("EXACT new best: {:.8f} at n={}".format(best_exact, n), flush=True)
                    sur_checked_s = sbest

            idx += 1
            if idx % 4 == 0:
                min_alloc = max(2.0, min(30.0, max(2.0, (phase_stop - time.monotonic()) / 4.0)))

        # At phase end, if a surrogate best was not exact-checked, do it if time remains.
        if sur_best_p is not None and sur_best_s < sur_checked_s and time.monotonic() < deadline - 5.0:
            seq = [float(x) for x in sur_best_p]
            es = exact_score(seq)
            if np.isfinite(es) and es < best_exact:
                best_exact = es
                best_seq = seq
                print("EXACT phase-end best: {:.8f} at n={}".format(best_exact, sur_best_n), flush=True)
            elif np.isfinite(es):
                sur_checked_s = min(sur_checked_s, sur_best_s)

    # Final exact polish around the best surrogate, if reasonable size and time remains.
    if sur_best_p is not None and sur_best_n <= 4096 and time.monotonic() < deadline - 12.0:
        print("Final exact polishing n={}".format(sur_best_n), flush=True)
        p_pol, e_pol = exact_polish(sur_best_p, sur_best_n, deadline - 1.5, rng)
        if np.isfinite(e_pol) and e_pol < best_exact:
            best_seq = [float(x) for x in p_pol]
            best_exact = e_pol
            print("Polish exact best: {:.8f}".format(best_exact), flush=True)

    # One last exact check of the best surrogate, in case it was never checked.
    if sur_best_p is not None and sur_best_s < sur_checked_s and time.monotonic() < deadline - 1.0:
        seq = [float(x) for x in sur_best_p]
        es = exact_score(seq)
        if np.isfinite(es) and es < best_exact:
            best_exact = es
            best_seq = seq
            print("Final exact best: {:.8f}".format(best_exact), flush=True)

    # Clean final sequence for the evaluator.
    out = []
    for x in best_seq:
        try:
            y = float(x)
        except Exception:
            y = 0.0
        if np.isfinite(y) and y > 0.0:
            out.append(y)
        else:
            out.append(0.0)

    if not out:
        out = [1.0]

    # Avoid any chance of the evaluator clipping to zero due to enormous values.
    m = max(out)
    if m > 100.0:
        scale = 100.0 / m
        out = [float(x) * scale for x in out]

    print("Returned exact best reference: {:.8f}, length={}".format(best_exact, len(out)), flush=True)
    return out
```
